# Task
You will be given a **Requirement** and a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML. Your task is to annotate every operation listed in **Operations** with a specification.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pseudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Take the principle of modularization into account.
2. For each operation, determine its functional boundary based on the given requirement. Ensure high cohesion and low coupling.
3. If there is any unspecified details, such as undefined constants, rates, and ratios, then you must infer them.
4. Make sure that all requirements can be covered.

# Requirement
```text
We want to automate the management of a small municipal library. The library has a name and manages a collection of documents and a set of registered members. Members have a first name and a surname. Documents are uniquely identified by their title. Every document has a title. A volume is a kind of document and also has an author. A book is a kind of volume and also has a bookId that identifies the physical or catalogued copy. A dictionary is a kind of volume and also has a dictionaryId that identifies the dictionary entry in the library catalog. A comic is also a kind of volume and has a recipient name. A journal is a kind of document and has a publication date. Only books can be borrowed.
A registered member can borrow or return a book, and the system must always be able to list the titles currently borrowed by that member. A member can hold at most three borrowed books at the same time. When a borrowing succeeds, the system creates a borrow record containing the borrowing date, the borrowed book, and a return due date exactly seven days later. A borrowed book can be extended on request when the request is made before the current due date. A return is accepted only when the member currently holds that book and the book is not overdue. The system records dates in the format "yyyy-MM-dd".
Functional requirement:
1. Register a new member. The library registers a new member from a first name and a surname. The first name and surname must not be null, empty, or whitespace-only. The library rejects a registration when another member already has exactly the same full name. A successful registration creates the member with no borrowed books and returns true. Any rejected registration returns false.

2. Borrow a book and create a record in the system. A member borrows a document by title. The library accepts the request only when the title exists, the document is a book, the book is not already borrowed by any member, and the member currently holds fewer than three books. A successful borrowing creates a borrow record with today's date as the borrowing date and a due date exactly seven days later, then returns true. Any rejected borrowing returns false.

3. Extend a borrowed record's return due date. A member may extend the due date of one of their own borrowed books by title. When today is on or after the borrowing date and strictly before the current due date, the library moves the due date forward by seven days and returns the new due date. Otherwise, the due date remains unchanged and the original due date is returned.

4. Return a book. A member returns a borrowed book by title. If the member does not currently hold that title, the operation returns false. If the borrowed book is overdue, the return is rejected, the borrow record remains active, and the operation returns false. Otherwise the borrow record is removed and the operation returns true.

5. List all borrowed book titles for a member. A member can list the titles of all currently borrowed books. If the member has no active borrowed books, the operation returns an empty list.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title municipalLibrary Model

class Book {
    bookId : String
}
class BorrowRecord {
    borrowingDate : Date
    returnDue : Date
    book : Book
    extendDueDate(today : Date) : Date
    isOverdue(today : Date) : boolean
}
class Comic {
    recipientName : String
}
class Dictionary {
    dictionaryId : String
}
abstract class Document {
    title : String
}
class Journal {
    publicationDate : Date
}
class Library {
    name : String
    documents : List<Document>
    members : List<Member>
    addDocument(doc : Document) : void
    registerMember(firstName : String, surname : String) : boolean
}
class Member {
    firstName : String
    surname : String
    borrowRecords : List<BorrowRecord>
    library : Library
    borrowBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
    extendReturnDueDate(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : Date
    listBorrowedBookTitles() : List<String>
    returnBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
}
abstract class Volume {
    author : String
}

Book --|> Volume
BorrowRecord --> Book : book
Comic --|> Volume
Dictionary --|> Volume
Journal --|> Document
Library *-- "*" Document : documents
Library *-- "*" Member : members  'opposite: library
Member *-- "*" BorrowRecord : borrowRecords
Member --> Library : library  'opposite: members
Volume --|> Document
@enduml
```

+ BorrowRecord::isOverdue
+ Library::addDocument
+ Library::registerMember
+ Member::borrowBook

+ Member::listBorrowedBookTitles
+ Member::returnBook

Borrow a book from the member's associated library using the book title.
Algorithm:
1. Verify that this member is associated with a library.
2. Search the library's documents for a document whose title exactly matches bookTitle.
3. Reject the request if:
   - no matching title exists,
   - the matching document is not a Book,
   - the book is already present in any member's active borrowRecords,
   - this member already holds 3 borrowed books.
4. If accepted:
   - create a new BorrowRecord
   - set borrowingDate = today
   - set returnDue = today + 7 days
   - set book = the matched Book
   - add the record to this member's borrowRecords
   - return true
5. Otherwise return false.
Related Features:
- firstName
- surname
- borrowRecords
- library
Related Classes: Member, Library, Document, Book, BorrowRecord

- bookTitle: String
  - Title of the book to borrow.
  - Must match a document title exactly.
- today: Date
  - The borrowing date, typically the current system date.
Output:
- boolean
  - true if the borrowing succeeds and a borrow record is created.
  - false if any acceptance condition fails.
Pre-condition:
- bookTitle is not null, empty, or whitespace-only.
- today is a valid date.
- this member has a valid library reference.
Post-condition:
- On success, this member gains one additional active borrow record for the specified book.

# Task
You will be given a **Requirement**, a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML, and a list of operation specifications. Your task is to correct the specifications that are inconsistent with the requirement.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pseudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Read the requirement and the class diagram.
2. For each operation specification, determine whether it is complete and consistent with the requirement.
3. If there is any error, correct the erroneous specification.
4. Follow the specification format. Output changed specifications only; OMIT unchanged specifications.

# Requirement
```text
We want to automate the management of a small municipal library. The library has a name and manages a collection of documents and a set of registered members. Members have a first name and a surname. Documents are uniquely identified by their title. Every document has a title. A volume is a kind of document and also has an author. A book is a kind of volume and also has a bookId that identifies the physical or catalogued copy. A dictionary is a kind of volume and also has a dictionaryId that identifies the dictionary entry in the library catalog. A comic is also a kind of volume and has a recipient name. A journal is a kind of document and has a publication date. Only books can be borrowed.
A registered member can borrow or return a book, and the system must always be able to list the titles currently borrowed by that member. A member can hold at most three borrowed books at the same time. When a borrowing succeeds, the system creates a borrow record containing the borrowing date, the borrowed book, and a return due date exactly seven days later. A borrowed book can be extended on request when the request is made before the current due date. A return is accepted only when the member currently holds that book and the book is not overdue. The system records dates in the format "yyyy-MM-dd".
Functional requirement:
1. Register a new member. The library registers a new member from a first name and a surname. The first name and surname must not be null, empty, or whitespace-only. The library rejects a registration when another member already has exactly the same full name. A successful registration creates the member with no borrowed books and returns true. Any rejected registration returns false.

2. Borrow a book and create a record in the system. A member borrows a document by title. The library accepts the request only when the title exists, the document is a book, the book is not already borrowed by any member, and the member currently holds fewer than three books. A successful borrowing creates a borrow record with today's date as the borrowing date and a due date exactly seven days later, then returns true. Any rejected borrowing returns false.

3. Extend a borrowed record's return due date. A member may extend the due date of one of their own borrowed books by title. When today is on or after the borrowing date and strictly before the current due date, the library moves the due date forward by seven days and returns the new due date. Otherwise, the due date remains unchanged and the original due date is returned.

4. Return a book. A member returns a borrowed book by title. If the member does not currently hold that title, the operation returns false. If the borrowed book is overdue, the return is rejected, the borrow record remains active, and the operation returns false. Otherwise the borrow record is removed and the operation returns true.

5. List all borrowed book titles for a member. A member can list the titles of all currently borrowed books. If the member has no active borrowed books, the operation returns an empty list.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title municipalLibrary Model

class Book {
    bookId : String
}
class BorrowRecord {
    borrowingDate : Date
    returnDue : Date
    book : Book
    extendDueDate(today : Date) : Date
    isOverdue(today : Date) : boolean
}
class Comic {
    recipientName : String
}
class Dictionary {
    dictionaryId : String
}
abstract class Document {
    title : String
}
class Journal {
    publicationDate : Date
}
class Library {
    name : String
    documents : List<Document>
    members : List<Member>
    addDocument(doc : Document) : void
    registerMember(firstName : String, surname : String) : boolean
}
class Member {
    firstName : String
    surname : String
    borrowRecords : List<BorrowRecord>
    library : Library
    borrowBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
    extendReturnDueDate(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : Date
    listBorrowedBookTitles() : List<String>
    returnBook(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : boolean
}
abstract class Volume {
    author : String
}

Book --|> Volume
BorrowRecord --> Book : book
Comic --|> Volume
Dictionary --|> Volume
Journal --|> Document
Library *-- "*" Document : documents
Library *-- "*" Member : members  'opposite: library
Member *-- "*" BorrowRecord : borrowRecords
Member --> Library : library  'opposite: members
Volume --|> Document
@enduml
```

# Operation Specifications
Member::borrowBook
Summary:
Borrow a book from the member's associated library using the book title.
Algorithm:
1. Verify that this member is associated with a library.
2. Search the library's documents for a document whose title exactly matches bookTitle.
3. Reject the request if:
   - no matching title exists,
   - the matching document is not a Book,
   - the book is already present in any member's active borrowRecords,
   - this member already holds 3 borrowed books.
4. If accepted:
   - create a new BorrowRecord
   - set borrowingDate = today
   - set returnDue = today + 7 days
   - set book = the matched Book
   - add the record to this member's borrowRecords
   - return true
5. Otherwise return false.
Related Features:
- firstName
- surname
- borrowRecords
- library
Related Classes: Member, Library, Document, Book, BorrowRecord
Input:
- bookTitle: String
  - Title of the book to borrow.
  - Must match a document title exactly.
- today: Date
  - The borrowing date, typically the current system date.
Output:
- boolean
  - true if the borrowing succeeds and a borrow record is created.
  - false if any acceptance condition fails.
Pre-condition:
- bookTitle is not null, empty, or whitespace-only.
- today is a valid date.
- this member has a valid library reference.
Post-condition:
- On success, this member gains one additional active borrow record for the specified book.
- The new record has borrowingDate = today and returnDue = today + 7 days.
- The book becomes borrowed by this member and cannot be borrowed by others while the record is active.
- On failure, no borrow record is added and no existing state changes.

Member::extendReturnDueDate
Summary:
Extend the due date of one of the member's own borrowed books by title.
Algorithm:
1. Find the active borrow record in borrowRecords whose book.title equals bookTitle.
2. If no such record exists, return the record's original due date is not possible; return the current date result as specified by the record logic, or if absent, no change cannot be applied.
3. If found, ask the borrow record to extend its due date using today.
4. Return the date produced by the borrow record.
Related Features:
- borrowRecords
- library
- firstName
- surname
Related Classes: Member, BorrowRecord, Book, Date
Input:
- bookTitle: String
  - The title of the borrowed book whose due date may be extended.
- today: Date
  - The date on which the extension request is made.
Output:
- Date
  - The new due date if the request is accepted by the borrow record.
  - Otherwise, the original due date of the matched record.
Pre-condition:
- bookTitle is not null, empty, or whitespace-only.
- today is a valid date.
- The member may or may not currently hold a matching borrowed book.
Post-condition:
- If a matching active borrow record exists and today is on or after the borrowing date and strictly before the due date, the due date is advanced by 7 days.
- Otherwise, the due date remains unchanged.
- No borrow record is created or removed.

Member::returnBook
Summary:
Return a borrowed book by title, rejecting overdue returns and returns for non-held titles.
Algorithm:
1. Find the active borrow record in borrowRecords whose book.title equals bookTitle.
2. If no matching record exists, return false.
3. Check whether the record is overdue on today using isOverdue(today).
4. If overdue, keep the record active and return false.
5. Otherwise remove the record from borrowRecords and return true.
Related Features:
- borrowRecords
- library
- firstName
- surname
Related Classes: Member, BorrowRecord, Book, Date
Input:
- bookTitle: String
  - Title of the borrowed book to be returned.
- today: Date
  - The date on which the return is attempted.
Output:
- boolean
  - true if the member currently holds the title and the book is not overdue.
  - false if the title is not held or the borrow is overdue.
Pre-condition:
- bookTitle is not null, empty, or whitespace-only.
- today is a valid date.
- this member has a borrowRecords collection initialized.
Post-condition:
- If the member did not hold the title, no state changes occur and false is returned.
- If the book is overdue, the borrow record remains active and false is returned.
- If the return succeeds, the matching borrow record is removed from borrowRecords and true is returned.

Member::listBorrowedBookTitles
Summary:
List the titles of all books currently borrowed by this member.
Algorithm:
1. Create an empty result list of strings.
2. For each active borrow record in borrowRecords:
   - read the associated book's title
   - append the title to the result list
3. Return the result list.
Related Features:
- borrowRecords
- library
- firstName
- surname
Related Classes: Member, BorrowRecord, Book, Document
Input:
- None
Output:
- List<String>
  - The titles of all currently borrowed books for this member.
  - The list is empty if the member has no active borrow records.
Pre-condition:
- this member exists.
- borrowRecords is initialized, possibly empty.
Post-condition:
- No state is modified.
- The returned list contains exactly the titles of books in this member's active borrowRecords, in the order they are stored.

Any rejected registration returns false.

```text
2. Borrow a book and create a record in the system. A member borrows a document by title. The library accepts the request only when the title exists, the document is a book, the book is not already borrowed by any member, and the member currently holds fewer than three books. A successful borrowing creates a borrow record with today's date as the borrowing date and a due date exactly seven days later, then returns true. Any rejected borrowing returns false.

3. Extend a borrowed record's return due date. A member may extend the due date of one of their own borrowed books by title. When today is on or after the borrowing date and strictly before the current due date, the library moves the due date forward by seven days and returns the new due date. Otherwise, the due date remains unchanged and the original due date is returned.

4. Return a book. A member returns a borrowed book by title. If the member does not currently hold that title, the operation returns false. If the borrowed book is overdue, the return is rejected, the borrow record remains active, and the operation returns false. Otherwise the borrow record is removed and the operation returns true.

5. List all borrowed book titles for a member. A member can list the titles of all currently borrowed books. If the member has no active borrowed books, the operation returns an empty list.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title municipalLibrary Model

class Book {
    bookId : String
}

    returnDue : Date

    recipientName : String

    dictionaryId : String

}

    publicationDate : Date

    firstName : String
    surname : String

    extendReturnDueDate(bookTitle : String, today : Date) : Date

    author : String
}

Journal --|> Document

Volume --|> Document
@enduml
```

# Operation Specifications
Library::addDocument
Summary:
Add a document to the library's managed collection.
Algorithm:
1. Check whether doc is already contained in documents.
2. If not present, append doc to documents.
3. If already present, keep the collection unchanged.
4. No explicit return value.
Related Features:
- name
- documents
- members
Related Classes: Document, Library
Input:
- doc: Document
  - The document to be stored in the library.
  - May be any concrete subtype of Document, including Book, Volume-derived types, or Journal.
Output:
- void
Pre-condition:
- doc is not null.
Post-condition:
- The library's documents collection contains doc.
- If doc was not already present, it is added exactly once.
- The members collection is not modified.

Library::registerMember
Summary:
Register a new member with a first name and surname, ensuring uniqueness by full name.
Algorithm:
1. Validate firstName and surname are not null, empty, or whitespace-only.
2. Build the candidate full name as "firstName surname" using exact string values.
3. Search members for an existing member with exactly the same firstName and surname.
4. If validation fails or a duplicate exists, return false.
5. Otherwise create a new Member with:
   - firstName = firstName
   - surname = surname
   - borrowRecords = empty list
   - library = this library
6. Add the new member to members.
7. Return true.
Related Features:
- name
- documents
- members
Related Classes: Library, Member
Input:
- firstName: String
  - The member's given name.
- surname: String
  - The member's family name.
Output:
- boolean
  - true if a new member is successfully registered.
  - false if input is invalid or another member already has the same full name.
Pre-condition:
- The library instance exists.
- firstName and surname are provided as string values.
Post-condition:
- If successful, a new Member is added to members with no borrowed books and its library reference set to this library.
- If unsuccessful, the members collection remains unchanged.

# Requirement
```text
We want to automate the management of a small municipal library. The library has a name and manages a collection of documents and a set of registered members. Members have a first name and a surname. Documents are uniquely identified by their title. Every document has a title. A volume is a kind of document and also has an author. A book is a kind of volume and also has a bookId that identifies the physical or catalogued copy. A dictionary is a kind of volume and also has a dictionaryId that identifies the dictionary entry in the library catalog. A comic is also a kind of volume and has a recipient name. A journal is a kind of document and has a publication date. Only books can be borrowed.
A registered member can borrow or return a book, and the system must always be able to list the titles currently borrowed by that member. A member can hold at most three borrowed books at the same time. When a borrowing succeeds, the system creates a borrow record containing the borrowing date, the borrowed book, and a return due date exactly seven days later. A borrowed book can be extended on request when the request is made before the current due date. A return is accepted only when the member currently holds that book and the book is not overdue. The system records dates in the format "yyyy-MM-dd".
Functional requirement:
1. Register a new member. The library registers a new member from a first name and a surname. The first name and surname must not be null, empty, or whitespace-only. The library rejects a registration when another member already has exactly the same full name. A successful registration creates the member with no borrowed books and returns true. Any rejected registration returns false.

2. Borrow a book and create a record in the system. A member borrows a document by title. The library accepts the request only when the title exists, the document is a book, the book is not already borrowed by any member, and the member currently holds fewer than three books. A successful borrowing creates a borrow record with today's date as the borrowing date and a due date exactly seven days later, then returns true. Any rejected borrowing returns false.

3. Extend a borrowed record's return due date. A member may extend the due date of one of their own borrowed books by title. When today is on or after the borrowing date and strictly before the current due date, the library moves the due date forward by seven days and returns the new due date. Otherwise, the due date remains unchanged and the original due date is returned.

4. Return a book. A member returns a borrowed book by title. If the member does not currently hold that title, the operation returns false. If the borrowed book is overdue, the return is rejected, the borrow record remains active, and the operation returns false. Otherwise the borrow record is removed and the operation returns true.

5. List all borrowed book titles for a member. A member can list the titles of all currently borrowed books. If the member has no active borrowed books, the operation returns an empty list.
```

# Operation Specifications
BorrowRecord::extendDueDate
Summary:
Extend the current return due date of this borrow record by exactly 7 days, based on the provided day.
Algorithm:
1. Read the current borrowingDate and returnDue of this borrow record.
2. If today is on or after borrowingDate and strictly before returnDue:
   - set returnDue := returnDue + 7 days
   - return the updated returnDue
3. Otherwise:
   - do not change returnDue
   - return the original returnDue
Related Features:
- borrowingDate
- returnDue
- book
Related Classes: Date, Book, BorrowRecord
Input:
- today: Date
  - The date when the extension request is made.
  - Must follow the system date type and be comparable with borrowingDate and returnDue.
Output:
- Date
  - The resulting due date after the operation.
  - If extension is accepted, this is 7 days later than the previous due date.
  - If extension is rejected, this is the unchanged original due date.
Pre-condition:
- today is a valid date value.
- borrowingDate and returnDue are valid date values.
- returnDue is not earlier than borrowingDate.
Post-condition:
- If today satisfies borrowingDate <= today < returnDue, returnDue is advanced by exactly 7 days.
- Otherwise, returnDue remains unchanged.
- The returned date equals the borrow record's current due date after the operation.

BorrowRecord::isOverdue
Summary:
Determine whether this borrow record is overdue on the given date.
Algorithm:
1. Compare today with returnDue.
2. Return true if today is strictly after returnDue.
3. Otherwise return false.
Related Features:
- borrowingDate
- returnDue
- book
Related Classes: Date, Book, BorrowRecord
Input:
- today: Date
  - The date used to check overdue status.
Output:
- boolean
  - true if the borrowed book is overdue on today; otherwise false.
Pre-condition:
- today is a valid date value.
- returnDue is a valid date value.
Post-condition:
- No state is modified.
- The returned value reflects whether today is after returnDue.

```result
 * revised description for operation_1
 */

/*
 * revised description for this operation_2
 */
```

Summary:
Extend the current return due date of this borrow record by exactly 7 days, based on the provided day.
Algorithm:
1. Read the current borrowingDate and returnDue of this borrow record.
2. If today is on or after borrowingDate and strictly before returnDue:
   - set returnDue := returnDue + 7 days
   - return the updated returnDue
3. Otherwise:
   - do not change returnDue
   - return the original returnDue
Related Features:
- borrowingDate
- returnDue
- book
Related Classes: Date, Book, BorrowRecord
Input:
- today: Date
  - The date when the extension request is made.
  - Must follow the system date type and be comparable with borrowingDate and returnDue.
Output:
- Date
  - The resulting due date after the operation.
  - If extension is accepted, this is 7 days later than the previous due date.
  - If extension is rejected, this is the unchanged original due date.
Pre-condition:
- today is a valid date value.
- borrowingDate and returnDue are valid date values.
- returnDue is not earlier than borrowingDate.
Post-condition:
- If today satisfies borrowingDate <= today < returnDue, returnDue is advanced by exactly 7 days.
- Otherwise, returnDue remains unchanged.
- The returned date equals the borrow record's current due date after the operation.

BorrowRecord::isOverdue
Summary:
Determine whether this borrow record is overdue on the given date.
Algorithm:
1. Compare today with returnDue.
2. Return true if today is strictly after returnDue.
3. Otherwise return false.
Related Features:
- borrowingDate
- returnDue
- book
Related Classes: Date, Book, BorrowRecord
Input:
- today: Date
  - The date used to check overdue status.
Output:
- boolean
  - true if the borrowed book is overdue on today; otherwise false.
Pre-condition:
- today is a valid date value.
- returnDue is a valid date value.
Post-condition:
- No state is modified.
- The returned value reflects whether today is after returnDue.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.

  1.2 Locate these files in your project.
  1.3 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.

  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;

  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the context.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

      return false;
    }

    for (Member member : getMembers())

    return true;

You will be given a **Java Class** generated from an Ecore model by EMF code generator with `suppressInterfaces=true`. Your task is to implement the Java methods unimplemented by taking additional contextual information into account.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.2 Locate these files in your project.
  1.3 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the context.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.2 Locate these files in your project.
  1.3 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the context.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

```java
   * Summary:
   * Borrow a book from the member's associated library using the book title.
   * Algorithm:
   * 1. Verify that this member is associated with a library.
   * 2. Search the library's documents for a document whose title exactly matches bookTitle.
   * 3. Reject the request if:
   *    - no matching title exists,
   *    - the matching document is not a Book,
   *    - the book is already present in any member's active borrowRecords,
   *    - this member already holds 3 borrowed books.
   * 4. If accepted:
   *    - create a new BorrowRecord
   *    - set borrowingDate = today
   *    - set returnDue = today + 7 days
   *    - set book = the matched Book
   *    - add the record to this member's borrowRecords
   *    - return true
   * 5. Otherwise return false.
   * Related Features:
   * - firstName
   * - surname
   * - borrowRecords
   * - library
   * Related Classes: Member, Library, Document, Book, BorrowRecord
   * Input:
   * - bookTitle: String
   *   - Title of the book to borrow.
   *   - Must match a document title exactly.
   * - today: Date
   *   - The borrowing date, typically the current system date.
   * Output:
   * - boolean
   *   - true if the borrowing succeeds and a borrow record is created.
   *   - false if any acceptance condition fails.
   * Pre-condition:
   * - bookTitle is not null, empty, or whitespace-only.
   * - today is a valid date.
   * - this member has a valid library reference.
   * Post-condition:
   * - On success, this member gains one additional active borrow record for the specified book.
   * - The new record has borrowingDate = today and returnDue = today + 7 days.
   * - The book becomes borrowed by this member and cannot be borrowed by others while the record is active.
   * - On failure, no borrow record is added and no existing state changes.
```

   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean borrowBook(final String bookTitle, final Date today)
  {
    if (bookTitle == null || bookTitle.trim().isEmpty() || today == null)
    {
      return false;
    }

    Library library = getLibrary();
    if (library == null)
    {
      return false;
    }

    Document matchedDocument = null;
    for (Document document : library.getDocuments())
    {
      if (document != null && bookTitle.equals(document.getTitle()))
      {
        matchedDocument = document;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (!(matchedDocument instanceof Book))
    {
      return false;
    }

    Book book = (Book)matchedDocument;

    for (Member member : library.getMembers())
    {
      for (BorrowRecord record : member.getBorrowRecords())
      {
        if (record.getBook() == book)
        {
          return false;
        }
      }
    }

    if (getBorrowRecords().size() >= 3)
    {
      return false;
    }

    BorrowRecord record = MunicipalLibraryFactory.eINSTANCE.createBorrowRecord();
    record.setBorrowingDate(today);
    Date dueDate = new Date(today.getTime());
    dueDate.setTime(dueDate.getTime() + 7L * 24L * 60L * 60L * 1000L);
    record.setReturnDue(dueDate);
    record.setBook(book);
    getBorrowRecords().add(record);
    return true;
    
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary:
   * Return a borrowed book by title, rejecting overdue returns and returns for non-held titles.
   * Algorithm:
   * 1. Find the active borrow record in borrowRecords whose book.title equals bookTitle.
   * 2. If no matching record exists, return false.
   * 3. Check whether the record is overdue on today using isOverdue(today).

   * - If the member did not hold the title, no state changes occur and false is returned.
   * - If the book is overdue, the borrow record remains active and false is returned.
   * - If the return succeeds, the matching borrow record is removed from borrowRecords and true is returned.

   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean returnBook(final String bookTitle, final Date today)
  {
    if (bookTitle == null || bookTitle.trim().isEmpty() || today == null)
    {
      return false;
    }

    for (BorrowRecord record : getBorrowRecords())
    {
      Book book = record.getBook();
      if (book != null && bookTitle.equals(book.getTitle()))
      {
        if (record.isOverdue(today))
        {
          return false;
        }
        return getBorrowRecords().remove(record);
      }
    }

    return false;
    
  }
